VIOLATION: halt-discipline-scenario-b-gap | MINOR severity. Spec § 7.1 scenario (b) "Baseline test count differs from 217 at session entry → HALT" requires a halt when pass/fail counts deviate from the spec's `tests=229 / pass=229 / fail=0` claim. At session entry the cluster-worktree baseline was `pass=216 / fail=1` (pre-R25, before new tests added), with the q01 AC-7 environmental failure pre-existing. The Implementer observed this (NEXT-ROLE.md line 180: "Pre-existing environmental note") and folded it into the ESCALATE-R25-01 DIAGNOSTIC as a subordinate note rather than as an independent scenario (b) halt. The operator dispositioned the environmental failure correctly via the ESCALATE block — but scenario (b) was not independently surfaced. Root cause: spec § 7.1 scenario (b) was worded narrowly ("count differs from 217") and the Implementer read total=217 as count-equal. Secondary root cause: Architect spec § 7.1 (b) did not specify pass/fail granularity. | R25 | IMPLEMENTER (primary) + ARCHITECT (secondary)

## Emerging patterns (R25 additions)

- R25 (wu-00-l0-contract, Phase 2 SLICE 3.A.5, full tier) delivers 12/12 ACs PASS + 1 PARTIAL + 2 PARTIAL (operator-dispositioned) with 0 CRITICAL / 3 MAJOR / 3 MINOR / 2 OBS; STATUS: MERGE-READY. All 3 MAJOR findings are spec/audit-trail drift, not functional defects. The L0 contract surface (`transformPair`, `RateSample`, `CounterMetadata`, synthetic counter generators) is functionally correct and test-backed.
- R25 is the first tessera multi-cluster round (wu-00-l0-contract cluster, Wave 1, parallel to wu-04-md-f4). The multi-cluster methodology introduced a new friction surface: cluster worktrees lack sibling repositories (no `../deploysignal`), causing q01 AC-7 to fail environmentally. This is a methodology-friction item, not an R25-specific failure — but Architects in cluster worktrees must empirically verify baseline pass/fail via `node --test` rather than inheriting reference-round attestations.
- R25 introduces a new MAJOR class: "spec-not-amended-post-disposition." Three spec sections (§ 5.1 AC-R25-14, § 3/§ 4.6/§ 9.6/§ 9.7 ALLOWED_SET, § 4.3/§ 5.1 AC-R25-12 tolerance) remain contradictory or stale at HEAD because the Architect-authored spec was not amended after operator dispositions. The ESCALATE mechanism surfaced the contradictions correctly; the amendment step was skipped. Future rounds: after any operator disposition of a spec-internal contradiction, the Architect must amend the non-authoritative sections before the round is marked ROUND-COMPLETE.
- The OBS-1 pattern (chore-B+ as a 3rd coordination commit) highlights that spec § 4.7 anticipates only chore-A + chore-B but not post-review Memorial entries committed to the branch. Future specs should either: (a) combine Memorial accretion into chore-B (before routing to Reviewer), or (b) explicitly anticipate a chore-B+ for Memorial entries after Reviewer routing.

Latent test-coverage gap; spec-prescription fidelity obligation requires implementing what the spec AC text says, including all asserted fields. CONFIRMATION: tactical-adjustment | One version-drift tactical fix applied to AC-R29-12 implementation: stripped `NODE_TEST_CONTEXT` and `NODE_TEST_WORKER_ID` from the subprocess environment (`env: subEnv`). Node.js v25.9.0 propagates these vars to child processes, causing Node.js's recursive-test-detection to silently skip the subprocess (exit 0, empty output). The spec prescribes `execFileSync('node', ['--test', '--test-reporter=tap', ...], { encoding: 'utf8' })` — this is extended with `env: strippedEnv` to make the spec's intended mechanism work in the installed Node.js version. Per CLAUDE-IMPLEMENTER.md TACTICAL AUTONOMY: "Spec API call uses a signature the installed version doesn't support → use the current signature." The REQUIREMENT (assert 243/241/2 on pre-R29 files) is satisfied; no observable-behavior change or scope change. | R29 | IMPLEMENTER

Architect spec § 9.2 R06 sweep claimed "all opts fields covered" without tracing through `parseNvlinkStatus`'s own defaults — a data-flow-level gap, not a syntax-level one. Matches `HardwareTopologySource` precedent so idiomatic; MERGE-READY; but the branch-binding coverage gate is structurally weaker than spec § 9.2 claims.
